# rq7-dev

RQ7, the training language, development data, three seeds each: cross-entropy trained
on Czech only (train-cs-ce) and on Slovak only (train-sk-ce), with about the same amount
of data (4 and 6 % fewer examples) and the same steps and hyperparameters as on both
(train-cs-sk-ce), against GLiNER zero-shot.
The per-dataset tables show how training on one language transfers to the other: CNEC
and OpenPII cs are Czech, UNER SNK and OpenPII sk Slovak. All three were trained on
MetaCentrum (fobos, NVIDIA L40S); the cs+sk model is its --tag gpu run, not the Mac's.
Train the nine models first (docs/metacentrum.md).

Stage: dev · commit c2b5bb68d5 · 2026-10-15T03:39:52+00:00 · seed 20261010, 1000 resamples · models: mdeberta-v3-base-tokenizer @ a0484667b2, gliner-multi-v2.1 @ 443d26d654, gliner-cs-sk-ce-gpu @ d7ade2e1a6, gliner-cs-sk-ce-gpu-s1 @ 51e354cfff, gliner-cs-sk-ce-gpu-s2 @ 9f4ede6fbc, gliner-cs-ce @ bc8d080c9a, gliner-cs-ce-s1 @ d644a320ab, gliner-cs-ce-s2 @ f555ca4bf9, gliner-sk-ce @ 3c04733043, gliner-sk-ce-s1 @ d40cda495b, gliner-sk-ce-s2 @ 42c32fbd58 · tool 0.6.0
